# Misc extra RIP events

namespace = tianxia

# IDs 1701-1800 reserved

##### Eternal Life

# Eternal Life reincarnation notification
character_event = {
	id = tianxia.1701
	desc = EVTDESC_TIANXIA_1701
	picture = GFX_evt_birth
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	immediate = {
		FROMFROM = {
			save_event_target_as = reborn_ancestor
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTA_TIANXIA_1701 # It is FROMFROM reborn!
		
		FROM = {
			dynasty = event_target:reborn_ancestor
			
			add_trait = legit_bastard
			
			if = {
				limit = {
					event_target:reborn_ancestor = {
						is_female = yes
					}
				}
				
				FROM = {
					set_mother = event_target:reborn_ancestor
				}
			}
			
			else = {
				set_father = event_target:reborn_ancestor
				set_real_father = event_target:reborn_ancestor
			}
			
			save_event_target_as = inheritance_sanity_check_character
			inheritance_sanity_check_effect = yes
		}
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 10
				trait = trusting
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 10
				trait = kind
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 10
				trait = humble
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTB_TIANXIA_1701 # A bastard, perhaps, but not a trueborn child!
		
		FROM = {
			dynasty = event_target:reborn_ancestor
			add_trait = bastard
			
			if = {
				limit = {
					event_target:reborn_ancestor = {
						is_female = yes
					}
				}
				
				FROM = {
					set_mother = event_target:reborn_ancestor
				}
			}
			
			else = {
				set_father = event_target:reborn_ancestor
				set_real_father = event_target:reborn_ancestor
			}
			
			save_event_target_as = inheritance_sanity_check_character
			inheritance_sanity_check_effect = yes
		}
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 10
				trait = trusting
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 10
				trait = proud
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTC_TIANXIA_1701 # That is not FROMFROM!
		
		FROM = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					event_target:reborn_ancestor = {
						is_female = no
					}
				}
				set_real_father = event_target:reborn_ancestor
			}
			
			save_event_target_as = inheritance_sanity_check_character
			inheritance_sanity_check_effect = yes
		}
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 10
				trait = paranoid
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 10
				trait = proud
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 100
				NOR = {
					AND = {
						has_secret_religion = no
						OR = {
							has_dharmic_religion_trigger = yes
							religion_openly_bon_or_reformed_trigger = yes
							religion_openly_west_african_or_reformed_trigger = yes
							religion = mazdaki
							religion = manichaean
							religion = khurmazta
							religion = druze
							religion_openly_yi_or_reformed_trigger = yes
							has_religion_feature = religion_feature_reincarnation
						}
					}
					has_secret_dharmic_religion_trigger = yes
					true_bon_religion_trigger = yes
					true_religion = mazdaki
					true_religion = manichaean
					true_religion = khurmazta
					true_religion = druze
					true_yi_religion_trigger = yes
					# TIANXIATODO: religion_feature_reincarnation check for secret religion; not straightforward
				}
			}
		}
	}
}



##### Meddling with vassals

### End War

# on_yearly_pulse for vassal, fire the event for the liege
character_event = {
	id = tianxia.1702
	hide_window = yes
	
	is_triggered_only = yes # on_yearly_pulse
	
	has_dlc = "Reapers"
	only_playable = yes
	war = yes
	
	trigger = {
		independent = no # Only vassals
		
		will_liege_enforce_peace = no
		has_liege_enforced_peace = no
		
		OR = {
			has_game_rule = {
				name = ai_vassal_meddling
				value = allowed
			}
			has_game_rule = {
				name = ai_vassal_meddling
				value = only_wars
			}
		}
		
		any_war = {
			OR = {
				AND = {
					attacker = {
						character = ROOT
					}
					defender = {
						liege = {
							any_vassal = {
								character = ROOT
							}
						}
					}
				}
				AND = {
					attacker = {
						liege = {
							any_vassal = {
								character = ROOT
							}
						}
					}
					defender = {
						character = ROOT
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	
	immediate = {
		#log = "[This.GetBestName] is a vassal at war with someone inside the realm!"
		
		if = {
			limit = {
				# Has a suitable liege
				liege = {
					ai = yes
					is_tribal = no
					is_nomadic = no
					OR = {
						prestige >= 200
						holds_favor_on = ROOT
					}
					is_actually_incapable_trigger = no
					prisoner = no
					in_seclusion = no
					has_regent = no
					NOR = {
						has_character_modifier = mandate_of_heaven_2
						has_character_modifier = mandate_of_heaven_1
					}
					NOR = {
						trait = under_regency
						trait = under_regency2
						trait = under_regency3
					}
					
					NOT = {
						reverse_has_opinion_modifier = {
							who = ROOT
							modifier = opinion_interfered_in_war
						}
					}
				}
				
				# Is at war with a fellow vassal
				any_war = {
					OR = {
						AND = {
							attacker = {
								character = ROOT
							}
							
							defender = {
								liege = {
									any_vassal = {
										character = ROOT
									}
									
									# The liege has a reason to care
									OR = {
										is_friend = PREV
										is_lover = PREV
										AND = {
											dynasty = PREV
											player_heir = {
												character = PREV
											}
										}
										is_close_relative = PREV
										is_rival = ROOT
										ROOT = {
											is_powerful_vassal = yes
											OR = {
												AND = {
													PREV = {
														real_tier = emperor
													}
													OR = {
														real_tier = king
														real_tier = duke
													}
												}
												AND = {
													PREV = {
														real_tier = king
													}
													real_tier = duke
												}
												PREV = {
													real_tier = duke
												}
											}
										}
										AND = {
											opinion = {
												who = PREV
												value >= 50
											}
											opinion = {
												who = ROOT
												value < -25
											}
										}
										AND = {
											opinion = {
												who = PREV
												value >= 25
											}
											opinion = {
												who = ROOT
												value < -50
											}
										}
									}
									NOR = {
										is_rival = PREV
										is_friend = ROOT
										is_lover = ROOT
										AND = {
											dynasty = ROOT
											player_heir = {
												character = ROOT
											}
										}
										is_close_relative = ROOT
									}
								}
							}
							
							war_score >= 50 # Winning the war
						}
						
						AND = {
							defender = {
								character = ROOT
							}
							
							attacker = {
								liege = {
									any_vassal = {
										character = ROOT
									}
									
									# The liege has a reason to care
									OR = {
										is_friend = PREV
										is_lover = PREV
										AND = {
											dynasty = PREV
											player_heir = {
												character = PREV
											}
										}
										is_close_relative = PREV
										is_rival = ROOT
										ROOT = {
											is_powerful_vassal = yes
											OR = {
												AND = {
													PREV = {
														real_tier = emperor
													}
													real_tier = king
												}
												AND = {
													PREV = {
														real_tier = king
													}
													real_tier = duke
												}
												PREV = {
													real_tier = duke
												}
											}
										}
										AND = {
											opinion = {
												who = PREV
												value >= 50
											}
											opinion = {
												who = ROOT
												value < -25
											}
										}
										AND = {
											opinion = {
												who = PREV
												value >= 25
											}
											opinion = {
												who = ROOT
												value < -50
											}
										}
									}
									NOR = {
										is_rival = PREV
										is_friend = ROOT
										is_lover = ROOT
										AND = {
											dynasty = ROOT
											player_heir = {
												character = ROOT
											}
										}
										is_close_relative = ROOT
									}
								}
							}
							
							war_score <= -50 # Winning the war
						}
					}
				}
			}
			
			random_list = {
				50 = {
					trigger = {
						liege = {
							any_vassal = {
								OR = {
									is_friend = PREV
									is_lover = PREV
									AND = {
										dynasty = PREV
										player_heir = {
											character = PREV
										}
									}
									is_close_relative = PREV
									liege = {
										OR = {
											is_rival = ROOT
											ROOT = {
												is_powerful_vassal = yes
												OR = {
													AND = {
														PREV = {
															real_tier = emperor
														}
														real_tier = king
													}
													AND = {
														PREV = {
															real_tier = king
														}
														real_tier = duke
													}
													PREV = {
														real_tier = duke
													}
												}
											}
											AND = {
												opinion = {
													who = PREV
													value >= 50
												}
												opinion = {
													who = ROOT
													value < -25
												}
											}
											AND = {
												opinion = {
													who = PREV
													value >= 25
												}
												opinion = {
													who = ROOT
													value < -50
												}
											}
										}
									}
								}
								
								liege = {
									NOR = {
										is_rival = PREV
										is_friend = ROOT
										is_lover = ROOT
										AND = {
											dynasty = ROOT
											player_heir = {
												character = ROOT
											}
										}
										is_close_relative = ROOT
									}
								}
								
								any_war = {
									attacker = {
										character = ROOT
									}
									defender = {
										character = PREV
									}
								}
							}
						}
					}
					
					liege = {
						any_vassal = {
							limit = {
								OR = {
									is_friend = PREV
									is_lover = PREV
									AND = {
										dynasty = PREV
										player_heir = {
											character = PREV
										}
									}
									is_close_relative = PREV
									liege = {
										OR = {
											is_rival = ROOT
											ROOT = {
												is_powerful_vassal = yes
												OR = {
													AND = {
														PREV = {
															real_tier = emperor
														}
														real_tier = king
													}
													AND = {
														PREV = {
															real_tier = king
														}
														real_tier = duke
													}
													PREV = {
														real_tier = duke
													}
												}
											}
											AND = {
												opinion = {
													who = PREV
													value >= 50
												}
												opinion = {
													who = ROOT
													value < -25
												}
											}
											AND = {
												opinion = {
													who = PREV
													value >= 25
												}
												opinion = {
													who = ROOT
													value < -50
												}
											}
										}
									}
								}
								
								liege = {
									NOR = {
										is_rival = PREV
										is_friend = ROOT
										is_lover = ROOT
										AND = {
											dynasty = ROOT
											player_heir = {
												character = ROOT
											}
										}
										is_close_relative = ROOT
									}
								}
								
								any_war = {
									attacker = {
										character = ROOT
									}
									defender = {
										character = PREV
									}
								}
							}
							
							save_event_target_as = target_defender
							
							ROOT = {
								save_event_target_as = target_vassal
								
								#log = "[This.Liege.GetBestName] might meddle in [This.GetBestName]'s wars!"
								#log = "[This.GetBestName] is attacking [target_defender.GetBestName]!"
								
								liege = {
									character_event = { id = tianxia.1703 } # Liege gets event
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
				
				50 = {
					trigger = {
						liege = {
							any_vassal = {
								OR = {
									is_friend = PREV
									is_lover = PREV
									AND = {
										dynasty = PREV
										player_heir = {
											character = PREV
										}
									}
									is_close_relative = PREV
									liege = {
										OR = {
											is_rival = ROOT
											ROOT = {
												is_powerful_vassal = yes
												OR = {
													AND = {
														PREV = {
															real_tier = emperor
														}
														real_tier = king
													}
													AND = {
														PREV = {
															real_tier = king
														}
														real_tier = duke
													}
													PREV = {
														real_tier = duke
													}
												}
											}
											AND = {
												opinion = {
													who = PREV
													value >= 50
												}
												opinion = {
													who = ROOT
													value < -25
												}
											}
											AND = {
												opinion = {
													who = PREV
													value >= 25
												}
												opinion = {
													who = ROOT

											}

									}
								}
								
								liege = {
									NOR = {
										is_rival = PREV
										is_friend = ROOT

										AND = {
											dynasty = ROOT

											}

# AI liege gets event - attacker in war
character_event = {
	id = tianxia.1703
	hide_window = yes
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				holds_favor_on = event_target:target_vassal
			}
			
			# Call in the Favor; the player doesn't get to pick the other option, so the AI also doesn't get it
			
			#log = "[This.GetBestName] meddled in [From.GetBestName]'s war against [target_defender.GetBestName] using a Favor!"
			
			event_target:target_vassal = {
				reverse_remove_favor = ROOT

				character_event = { id = tianxia.1704 } # Ping
			}
		}
		
		else = {
			random_list = {
				50 = {
					mult_modifier = {
						factor = 1.5
						opinion = {
							who = event_target:target_defender
							value >= 25
						}
					}
					
					mult_modifier = {
						factor = 1.5
						opinion = {
							who = event_target:target_defender
							value >= 50
						}
					}
					
					mult_modifier = {
						factor = 1.5
						opinion = {
							who = event_target:target_defender
							value >= 75
						}
					}
					
					mult_modifier = {
						factor = 1.5
						opinion = {
							who = event_target:target_vassal
							value < -25
						}
					}
					
					mult_modifier = {
						factor = 1.5
						opinion = {
							who = event_target:target_vassal
							value < -50
						}
					}
					
					mult_modifier = {
						factor = 1.5
						opinion = {
							who = event_target:target_vassal
							value < -75
						}
					}
					
					# Meddle
					prestige = -100
					
					reverse_opinion = {
						name = opinion_interfered_in_war
						who = event_target:target_vassal
						years = 5
					}
					
					#log = "[This.GetBestName] meddled in [From.GetBestName]'s war against [target_defender.GetBestName]!"
					
					event_target:target_vassal = {
						character_event = { id = tianxia.1705 } # Ping
					}
				}
				
				50 = {
					mult_modifier = {
						factor = 1.5
						opinion = {
							who = event_target:target_vassal
							value >= 25
						}
					}
					
					mult_modifier = {
						factor = 1.5
						opinion = {
							who = event_target:target_vassal
							value >= 50
						}
					}
					
					mult_modifier = {
						factor = 1.5
						opinion = {
							who = event_target:target_vassal
							value >= 75
						}
					}
					
					mult_modifier = {
						factor = 1.5
						opinion = {
							who = event_target:target_defender
							value < -25
						}
					}
					
					mult_modifier = {
						factor = 1.5
						opinion = {
							who = event_target:target_defender
							value < -50
						}
					}
					
					mult_modifier = {
						factor = 1.5
						opinion = {
							who = event_target:target_defender
							value < -75
						}
					}
					
					# Do nothing
					
					#log = "[This.GetBestName] did not meddle in [From.GetBestName]'s war against [target_defender.GetBestName]!"
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# Ping to get the correct scopes - Favor
character_event = {
	id = tianxia.1704
	hide_window = yes
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	immediate = {
		letter_event = {
			id = RIP.30010
			days = 3
		}
	}
}

# Ping to get the correct scopes - no Favor
character_event = {
	id = tianxia.1705
	hide_window = yes
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	immediate = {
		letter_event = {
			id = RIP.30000
			days = 3
		}
	}
}

# AI liege gets event - defender in war
character_event = {
	id = tianxia.1706
	hide_window = yes
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				holds_favor_on = event_target:target_vassal
			}
			
			# Call in the Favor; the player doesn't get to pick the other option, so the AI also doesn't get it
			
			#log = "[This.GetBestName] meddled in [From.GetBestName]'s war against [target_attacker.GetBestName] using a Favor!"
			
			event_target:target_vassal = {
				reverse_remove_favor = ROOT

				character_event = { id = tianxia.1707 } # Ping
			}
		}
		
		else = {
			random_list = {
				50 = {
					mult_modifier = {
						factor = 1.5
						opinion = {
							who = event_target:target_attacker
							value >= 25
						}
					}
					
					mult_modifier = {
						factor = 1.5
						opinion = {
							who = event_target:target_attacker
							value >= 50
						}
					}
					
					mult_modifier = {
						factor = 1.5
						opinion = {
							who = event_target:target_attacker
							value >= 75
						}
					}
					
					mult_modifier = {
						factor = 1.5
						opinion = {
							who = event_target:target_vassal
							value < -25
						}
					}
					
					mult_modifier = {
						factor = 1.5
						opinion = {
							who = event_target:target_vassal
							value < -50
						}
					}
					
					mult_modifier = {
						factor = 1.5
						opinion = {
							who = event_target:target_vassal
							value < -75
						}
					}
					
					# Meddle
					prestige = -200
					
					#log = "[This.GetBestName] meddled in [From.GetBestName]'s war against [target_attacker.GetBestName]!"
					
					reverse_opinion = {
						name = opinion_interfered_in_war
						who = event_target:target_vassal
						years = 5
					}
					
					event_target:target_vassal = {
						character_event = { id = tianxia.1708 } # Ping
					}
				}
				
				50 = {
					mult_modifier = {
						factor = 1.5
						opinion = {
							who = event_target:target_vassal
							value >= 25
						}
					}
					
					mult_modifier = {
						factor = 1.5
						opinion = {
							who = event_target:target_vassal
							value >= 50
						}
					}
					
					mult_modifier = {
						factor = 1.5
						opinion = {
							who = event_target:target_vassal
							value >= 75
						}
					}
					
					mult_modifier = {
						factor = 1.5
						opinion = {
							who = event_target:target_attacker
							value < -25
						}
					}
					
					mult_modifier = {
						factor = 1.5
						opinion = {
							who = event_target:target_attacker
							value < -50
						}
					}
					
					mult_modifier = {
						factor = 1.5
						opinion = {
							who = event_target:target_attacker
							value < -75
						}
					}
					
					# Do nothing
					
					#log = "[This.GetBestName] did not meddle in [From.GetBestName]'s war against [target_attacker.GetBestName]!"
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

### Demand Gavelkind

# on_decade_pulse for vassal, fire the event for the liege
character_event = {
	id = tianxia.1709
	hide_window = yes
	
	is_triggered_only = yes # on_decade_pulse
	
	has_dlc = "Reapers"
	only_playable = yes
	
	trigger = {
		independent = no
		
		is_feudal = yes
		mercenary = no
		holy_order = no
		is_vice_royalty = no
		is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
		
		OR = {
			has_game_rule = {
				name = ai_vassal_meddling
				value = allowed
			}
			has_game_rule = {
				name = ai_vassal_meddling
				value = only_gavelkind
			}
		}
		
		liege = {
			is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
			has_regent = no
			NOR = {
				has_character_modifier = mandate_of_heaven_2
				has_character_modifier = mandate_of_heaven_1
			}
			NOR = {
				trait = under_regency
				trait = under_regency2
				trait = under_regency3
			}
			
			NOT =  {
				reverse_has_opinion_modifier = {
					name = opinion_demanded_succ_law_change
					who = ROOT
				}
			}
			
			prestige >= 100
		}
		
		any_demesne_title = {
			tier = ROOT
			OR = {
				controls_religion = no
				is_primary_holder_title = yes
			}
			is_law_potential = succ_gavelkind
			NOT = {
				has_law = succ_gavelkind
			}
		}
		
		# Don't shoot yourself in the foot!
		NOR = {
			is_friend = liege
			is_lover = liege
			AND = {
				dynasty = liege
				OR = {
					is_close_relative = liege
					NOT = {
						is_rival = liege
					}
				}
			}
			any_demesne_title = {
				tier = ROOT
				current_heir = {
					any_vassal = {
						character = ROOT
					}
				}
			}
			liege = {
				OR = {
					dynasty = ROOT
					is_parent_of = ROOT
				}
				player_heir = {
					character = ROOT
				}
			}
		}
	}
	
	immediate = {
		#log = "[This.Liege.GetBestName] might demand Gavelkind from [This.GetBestName]!"
		
		liege = {
			character_event = { id = tianxia.1710 }
		}
	}
}

# AI liege gets event
character_event = {
	id = tianxia.1710
	hide_window = yes
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				holds_favor_on = FROM
			}
			
			# Call in the Favor; the player doesn't get to pick the other option, so the AI also doesn't get it
			random_list = {
				50 = {
					mult_modifier = {
						factor = 1.5
						opinion = {
							who = FROM
							value < -25
						}
					}
					
					mult_modifier = {
						factor = 1.5
						opinion = {
							who = FROM
							value < -50
						}
					}
					
					mult_modifier = {
						factor = 1.5
						opinion = {
							who = FROM
							value < -75
						}
					}
					
					mult_modifier = {
						factor = 0.1
						NOR = {
							is_rival = FROM
							FROM = {
								is_powerful_vassal = yes
								OR = {
									AND = {
										ROOT = {
											real_tier = emperor
										}
										OR = {
											real_tier = king
											real_tier = duke
										}
									}
									AND = {
										ROOT = {
											real_tier = king
										}
										real_tier = duke
									}
									ROOT = {
										real_tier = duke
									}
								}
							}
							reverse_opinion = {
								who = FROM
								value < 0
							}
						}
					}
					
					# Meddle
					prestige = -100
					
					#log = "[This.GetBestName] demanded Gavelkind from [From.GetBestName] using a Favor!"
					
					FROM = {
						random_demesne_title = {
							limit = {
								tier = PREV
								OR = {
									controls_religion = no
									is_primary_holder_title = yes
								}
								NOT = {
									has_law = succ_gavelkind
								}
							}
							
							preferred_limit = {
								is_primary_holder_title = no
							}
							
							save_event_target_as = gavelkind_title
						}
					
						reverse_remove_favor = ROOT
					}
					
					reverse_opinion = {
						name = opinion_demanded_succ_law_change
						who = FROM
						years = 5
					}
					
					FROM = {
						character_event = { id = tianxia.1711 } # Ping
					}
				}
				
				50 = {
					mult_modifier = {
						factor = 1.5
						opinion = {
							who = FROM
							value >= 25
						}
					}
					
					mult_modifier = {
						factor = 1.5
						opinion = {
							who = FROM
							value >= 50
						}
					}
					
					mult_modifier = {
						factor = 1.5
						opinion = {
							who = FROM
							value >= 75
						}
					}
					
					# Do nothing
					
					#log = "[This.GetBestName] did not demand Gavelkind from [From.GetBestName]!"
				}
			}
		}
		
		else = {
			random_list = {
				50 = {
					mult_modifier = {
						factor = 1.5
						opinion = {
							who = FROM
							value < -25
						}
					}
					
					mult_modifier = {
						factor = 1.5
						opinion = {
							who = FROM
							value < -50
						}
					}
					
					mult_modifier = {
						factor = 1.5
						opinion = {
							who = FROM
							value < -75
						}
					}
					
					mult_modifier = {
						factor = 0.1
						NOR = {
							is_rival = FROM
							FROM = {
								is_powerful_vassal = yes
								OR = {
									AND = {
										ROOT = {
											real_tier = emperor
										}
										OR = {
											real_tier = king
											real_tier = duke
										}
									}
									AND = {
										ROOT = {
											real_tier = king
										}
										real_tier = duke
									}
									ROOT = {
										real_tier = duke
									}
								}
							}
							reverse_opinion = {
								who = FROM
								value < 0
							}
						}
					}
					
					# Meddle
					prestige = -100

##### Prosperity modifiers

# Silk Road Market
province_event = {
	id = tianxia.1713
	title = PROSPERITY_TITLE
	desc = EVTDESC_TIANXIA_1713
	picture = GFX_evt_prospering_province
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_economy

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTA_TIANXIA_1713

		owner = {
			show_scope_change = no

			scaled_wealth = {
				value = -0.5
				min = -20
				max = -100
			}
		}

		add_province_modifier = {
			name = silk_road_market
			duration = -1
		}

		ai_chance = {
			factor = 95
			
			trigger = {
				owner = {
					scaled_wealth = 0.5
					NOT = {
						trait = content
					}
				}
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 10
				owner = {
					trait = greedy
				}
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTB_TIANXIA_1713

		ai_chance = {
			factor = 5
		}
	}
}

### Expansion of Court Physician flavor events

# Hidden setup for liege
character_event = {
	id = tianxia.1735
	hide_window = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes # on_yearly_pulse, random_events
	
	only_playable = yes
	has_dlc = "Reapers"
	min_age = 16
	#only_capable = yes
	prisoner = no

	trigger = {
		is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
		#has_assigned_minor_title = title_court_physician
		any_courtier_or_vassal = {
			has_minor_title = title_court_physician
			is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
		}
		is_tribal = no
		is_nomadic = no

		trigger_if = {
			limit = { health >= 5 }

			OR = {
				trait = stressed
				trait = diligent
				trait = wroth
			}
		}

		NOR = {
			trait = slothful
			has_character_flag = stress_relief
			has_global_flag = ck2_tutorial_2 # Not in the learning scenario
		}

		any_courtier_or_vassal = {
			has_minor_title = title_court_physician
			is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no

			OR = {
				has_character_flag = is_court_physician
				trait = physician
			}
		}
	}

	weight_multiplier = {
		factor = 1

		mult_modifier = {
			factor = 2
			trait = diligent
		}

		mult_modifier = {
			factor = 2
			trait = stressed
		}

		mult_modifier = {
			factor = 1.5

			any_courtier_or_vassal = {
				has_minor_title = title_court_physician
				learning >= 16
			}
		}

		mult_modifier = {
			factor = 0.2

			any_courtier_or_vassal = {
				has_minor_title = title_court_physician
				learning < 14
			}
		}

		mult_modifier = {
			factor = 0.1

			any_courtier_or_vassal = {
				has_minor_title = title_court_physician
				learning < 10
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		random_courtier_or_vassal = {
			limit = { has_minor_title = title_court_physician }
			save_event_target_as = physician_target
			
			character_event = { id = tianxia.1736 } # Check with Court Physician if they want to do this
		}
	}
}

# Suggest liege takes it easy?

		}

			factor = 100

					is_rival = FROM
				}
			}

			}
		}
	}

		piety = -25
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			
			trigger = {
				NAND = {
					OR = {
						is_friend = FROM
						is_lover = FROM
					}
					trait = diligent
					NOT = {
						trait = arbitrary
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# Setup for liege
character_event = {
	id = tianxia.1742
	hide_window = yes
	
	is_triggered_only = yes # on_yearly_pulse, random_events

	only_playable = yes
	has_dlc = "Reapers"
	min_age = 16
	#only_capable = yes
	prisoner = no
	war = no

	trigger = {
		is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
		is_tribal = no
		is_nomadic = no
		
		any_demesne_province = {
			owned_by = ROOT
			hospital_level >= 1

			OR = {
				hospital_has_building = library_1
				hospital_has_building = pharmacology_laboratory_1
				hospital_has_building = medical_academy_1
			}
		}

		any_courtier_or_vassal = {
			is_tribal = no
			is_nomadic = no
			has_minor_title = title_court_physician
			NOT = { has_character_flag = spent_time_at_hospital }
			is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		random_demesne_province = {
			limit = {
				owned_by = ROOT
				hospital_level >= 1

				OR = {
					hospital_has_building = library_1
					hospital_has_building = pharmacology_laboratory_1
					hospital_has_building = medical_academy_1
				}
			}
			save_event_target_as = hospital_location
		}

		random_courtier_or_vassal = {
			limit = { has_minor_title = title_court_physician }
			save_event_target_as = learning_physician
			
			character_event = { id = tianxia.1743 } # Let the physician decide if they want to ask
		}
	}
}

	id = tianxia.1744
	desc = EVTDESC_TIANXIA_1744
	picture = GFX_evt_court_physician
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTA_TIANXIA_1744 # Great!

		if = {
			limit = { trait = physician }

			change_learning = 2
			set_character_flag = physician_got_learning
		}
		else = {
			add_trait = physician
			set_character_flag = physician_got_trait
		}
		
		hidden_tooltip = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					society_member_of = the_alchemists
				}
				
				change_variable = {
					which = alchemist_healing_knowledge
					value = 5
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# Phantom pain research
character_event = {
	id = tianxia.1747
	desc = EVTDESC_TIANXIA_1747
	picture = GFX_evt_court_physician
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	immediate = {
		clr_character_flag = phantom_pain_help
		clr_character_flag = phantom_pain_hurt
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTA_TIANXIA_1747 # I'll help!
		
		set_character_flag = phantom_pain_help
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			
			trigger = {
				NOT = {
					is_rival = FROM
					trait = cruel
				}
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 10
				trait = kind
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 5
				trait = just
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 2
				trait = honest
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 2
				opinion = {
					who = FROM
					value >= 25
				}
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 2
				opinion = {
					who = FROM
					value >= 50
				}
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 2
				opinion = {
					who = FROM
					value >= 75
				}
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTB_TIANXIA_1747 # I'll hurt the fool!
		
		set_character_flag = phantom_pain_hurt
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			
			trigger = {
				NOR = {
					is_friend = FROM
					is_lover = FROM
				}
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 10
				trait = cruel
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 5
				trait = arbitrary
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 2
				trait = deceitful
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 2
				opinion = {
					who = FROM
					value < -25
				}
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 2
				opinion = {
					who = FROM
					value < -50
				}
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 2
				opinion = {
					who = FROM
					value < -75
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# Inform physician - extreme treatment failed
character_event = {
	id = tianxia.1750
	desc = EVTDESC_TIANXIA_1750
	picture = GFX_evt_court_physician
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTA_TIANXIA_1750 # I did say it was experimental!
		
		prestige = -100
		
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_dlc = "Jade Dragon"
				e_china = {
					holder_scope = {
						is_chinese_emperor_trigger = yes
						OR = {
							character = FROM
							AND = {
								is_close_relative = FROM
								dynasty = FROM
							}
						}
						NOT = {
							character = ROOT
						}
					}
				}
			}
			
			if = {
				limit = {
					FROM = {
						is_chinese_emperor_trigger = yes
					}
				}
				detract_grace_super_huge_effect = yes
			}
			else = {
				detract_grace_massive_effect = yes
			}
		}
	}
}

### Treatment events

# Sicky child - help or "help"?
character_event = {
	id = tianxia.1756
	desc = EVTDESC_TIANXIA_1756
	picture = GFX_evt_birth
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	trigger = {
		has_minor_title = title_court_physician
		OR = {
			vassal_of = event_target:host_target
			host = {
				character = event_target:host_target
			}
		}
		event_target:host_target = {
			is_alive = yes
		}
		event_target:sickly_infant = {
			is_alive = yes
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTA_TIANXIA_1756 # I will offer to help!
		
		opinion = {
			who = event_target:sickly_infant
			modifier = opinion_help_sickly_infant
		}
		
		event_target:infant_guardian = {
			character_event = {
				id = RIP.11067
				days = 6
			}
		}
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			
			trigger = {
				NOR = {
					AND = {
						event_target:sickly_infant = {
							father = {
								is_rival = ROOT
							}
							NOT = {
								real_father = {
									character = ROOT
								}
							}
							mother = {
								is_rival = ROOT
							}
						}
						trait = deceitful
						trait = cruel
					}
					
					AND = {
						event_target:sickly_infant = {
							father = {
								NOR = {
									is_friend = ROOT
									is_lover = ROOT
								}
							}
							NOT = {
								real_father = {
									character = ROOT
								}
							}
							mother = {
								NOR = {
									is_friend = ROOT
									is_lover = ROOT
								}
							}
							OR = {
								mother = {
									any_demesne_title = {
										OR = {
											tier = PREV
											tier = king
											controls_religion = yes
										}
										OR = {
											claimed_by = ROOT
											any_pretender = {
												character = ROOT
											}
										}
									}
								}
								father = {
									any_demesne_title = {
										OR = {
											tier = PREV
											tier = king
											controls_religion = yes
										}
										OR = {
											claimed_by = ROOT
											any_pretender = {
												character = ROOT
											}
										}
									}
								}
							}
						}
						trait = ambitious
						trait = deceitful
						NOT = {
							trait = kind
						}
					}
				}
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 100
				trait = kind
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 10
				trait = honest
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 10
				trait = just
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 2
				opinion = {
					who = event_target:infant_guardian
					value >= 25
				}
				event_target:sickly_infant = {
					is_child_of = event_target:infant_guardian
				}
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 5
				opinion = {
					who = event_target:infant_guardian
					value >= 50
				}
				event_target:sickly_infant = {
					is_child_of = event_target:infant_guardian
				}
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 10
				opinion = {
					who = event_target:infant_guardian
					value >= 75
				}
				event_target:sickly_infant = {
					is_child_of = event_target:infant_guardian
				}
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				event_target:sickly_infant = {
					OR = {
						father = {
							character = ROOT
						}
						real_father = {
							character = ROOT
						}
						mother = {
							character = ROOT
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		
		name = EVTOPTB_TIANXIA_1756 # It would be such a shame if the child died...
		
		opinion = {
			who = event_target:sickly_infant
			modifier = opinion_harm_sickly_infant
		}
		
		event_target:infant_guardian = {
			character_event = {
				id = RIP.11067
				days = 6
			}
		}
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			
			trigger = {
				NOT = {
					event_target:sickly_infant = {
						OR = {
							father = {
								OR = {
									character = ROOT
									is_friend = ROOT
									is_lover = ROOT
								}
							}
							mother = {
								OR = {
									character = ROOT
									is_friend = ROOT
									is_lover = ROOT
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
				OR = {
					trait = cruel
					trait = arbitrary
					trait = deceitful
					AND = {
						event_target:sickly_infant = {
							father = {
								NOR = {
									is_friend = ROOT
									is_lover = ROOT
								}
							}
							NOT = {
								real_father = {
									character = ROOT
								}
							}
							mother = {
								NOR = {
									is_friend = ROOT
									is_lover = ROOT
								}
							}
							OR = {
								mother = {
									any_demesne_title = {
										OR = {
											tier = PREV
											tier = king
											controls_religion = yes
										}
										OR = {
											claimed_by = ROOT
											any_pretender = {
												character = ROOT
											}
										}
									}
								}
								father = {
									any_demesne_title = {
										OR = {
											tier = PREV
											tier = king
											controls_religion = yes
										}
										OR = {
											claimed_by = ROOT
											any_pretender = {
												character = ROOT
											}
										}
									}
								}
							}
						}
						trait = ambitious
					}
				}
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 10
				trait = cruel
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 10
				trait = arbitrary
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 10
				trait = deceitful
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 2
				opinion = {
					who = event_target:infant_guardian
					value < -25
				}
				event_target:sickly_infant = {
					is_child_of = event_target:infant_guardian
				}
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 5
				opinion = {
					who = event_target:infant_guardian
					value < -50
				}
				event_target:sickly_infant = {
					is_child_of = event_target:infant_guardian

				}
				event_target:sickly_infant = {
					is_child_of = event_target:infant_guardian
				}
			}
		}

# Court Physician needs to give a diagnosis - be honest or not?
character_event = {
	id = tianxia.1763
	desc = EVTDESC_TIANXIA_1763
	picture = GFX_evt_court_physician
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	trigger = {
		OR = { # We are still expected to treat the character
			is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
			AND = {
				is_actually_incapable_trigger = no
				prisoner = no
				in_seclusion = yes
				host = {
					OR = {
						character = FROM
						any_courtier = {
							is_actually_incapable_trigger = no
							prisoner = no
							in_seclusion = yes
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		FROM = {
			has_any_symptom = yes
		}
		has_minor_title = title_court_physician
	}

	immediate = {
		FROM = { # Vanilla logic from RIP.11045, minus the opinion bit as that's handled differently
			clr_character_flag = fake_diagnosis
			
			clear_flags_with_prefix = diagnosis_is_
			
			random_list = {
				40 = {
					mult_modifier = {
						factor = 2
						num_of_symptoms >= 2
					}

					mult_modifier = {
						factor = 2
						num_of_symptoms >= 3
					}

					mult_modifier = {
						factor = 1.5

						event_target:court_physician = {
							OR = {
								trait = scholar
								trait = mutazilite
							}
						}
					}

					mult_modifier = {
						factor = 1.2
						event_target:court_physician = { is_smart_trigger = yes }
					}

					mult_modifier = {
						factor = 5

						# Horse physicians are always right in regular starts
						event_target:court_physician = {
							culture = horse_culture

							NOR = {
								has_alternate_start_parameter = {
									key = special_culture
									value = animal_kingdoms
								}

								has_alternate_start_parameter = {
									key = special_culture
									value = animal_world
								}
							}
						}
					}

					mult_modifier = {
						factor = 1.2
						event_target:court_physician = { physician_good_trigger = yes }
					}

					mult_modifier = {
						factor = 1.2
						event_target:court_physician = { learning >= 25 }
					}

					mult_modifier = {
						factor = 1.2
						event_target:court_physician = { learning >= 20 }
					}

					mult_modifier = {
						factor = 1.2
						event_target:court_physician = { learning >= 15 }
					}

					mult_modifier = {
						factor = 0.8
						event_target:court_physician = { learning < 15 }
					}

					mult_modifier = {
						factor = 0.8
						event_target:court_physician = { learning < 10 }
					}

					set_character_flag = correct_diagnosis
				}

				60 = {
					trigger = {
						# Horse physicians are always right in regular starts
						event_target:court_physician = {
							culture = horse_culture

							NOR = {
								has_alternate_start_parameter = {
									key = special_culture
									value = animal_kingdoms
								}

								has_alternate_start_parameter = {
									key = special_culture
									value = animal_world
								}
							}
						}
					}

					mult_modifier = {
						factor = 1.2
						event_target:court_physician = { is_dumb_trigger = yes }
					}

					mult_modifier = {
						factor = 1.2
						event_target:court_physician = { physician_bad_trigger = yes }
					}

					mult_modifier = {
						factor = 0.8
						event_target:court_physician = { learning >= 25 }
					}

					mult_modifier = {
						factor = 0.8
						event_target:court_physician = { learning >= 20 }
					}

					mult_modifier = {
						factor = 0.8
						event_target:court_physician = { learning >= 15 }
					}

					mult_modifier = {
						factor = 1.2
						event_target:court_physician = { learning < 15 }
					}

					mult_modifier = {
						factor = 1.2
						event_target:court_physician = { learning < 10 }
					}

					set_character_flag = faulty_diagnosis
				}
			}

			# Now we check to see what disease the character has
			random_list = {
				10 = {
					trigger = {
						trigger_if = {
							limit = { has_character_flag = correct_diagnosis }
							has_character_flag = got_consumption
						}
						trigger_else = {
							NOT = { has_character_flag = got_consumption }
						}
					}

					if = { # Horse physicians are always right in regular starts
						limit = {
							event_target:court_physician = {
								culture = horse_culture

								NOR = {
									has_alternate_start_parameter = {
										key = special_culture
										value = animal_kingdoms
									}

									has_alternate_start_parameter = {
										key = special_culture
										value = animal_world
									}
								}
							}
						}

						set_character_flag = diagnosis_is_consumption_flag_horse
					}
					else_if = { # Really good doctors know for sure
						limit = {
							has_character_flag = correct_diagnosis

							event_target:court_physician = {
								trigger_if = {
									limit = { physician_good_trigger = yes }
									learning >= 20
								}
								trigger_else = {
									learning >= 25
								}
							}
						}

						set_character_flag = diagnosis_is_consumption_flag_certain
					}
					else_if = { # Good doctors are pretty sure
						limit = {
							event_target:court_physician = {
								trigger_if = {
									limit = { physician_good_trigger = yes }
									learning >= 15
								}
								trigger_else = {
									learning >= 20
								}
							}
						}

						set_character_flag = diagnosis_is_consumption_flag_sure
					}
					else_if = { # Awful doctors are uncertain
						limit = {
							event_target:court_physician = {
								trigger_if = {
									limit = { physician_bad_trigger = yes }
									learning < 15
								}
								trigger_else = {
									learning < 10
								}
							}
						}

						set_character_flag = diagnosis_is_consumption_flag_uncertain
					}
					else = { # Otherwise, doctors are fairly sure
						set_character_flag = diagnosis_is_consumption_flag
					}
				}

				10 = {
					trigger = {
						trigger_if = {
							limit = { has_character_flag = correct_diagnosis }
							has_character_flag = got_slow_fever
						}
						trigger_else = {
							NOT = { has_character_flag = got_slow_fever }
						}
					}

					if = { # Horse physicians are always right in regular starts
						limit = {
							event_target:court_physician = {
								culture = horse_culture

								NOR = {
									has_alternate_start_parameter = {
										key = special_culture
										value = animal_kingdoms
									}

									has_alternate_start_parameter = {
										key = special_culture
										value = animal_world
									}
								}
							}
						}

						set_character_flag = diagnosis_is_slow_fever_flag_horse
					}
					else_if = { # Really good doctors know for sure
						limit = {
							has_character_flag = correct_diagnosis

							event_target:court_physician = {
								trigger_if = {
									limit = { physician_good_trigger = yes }
									learning >= 20
								}
								trigger_else = {
									learning >= 25
								}
							}
						}

						set_character_flag = diagnosis_is_slow_fever_flag_certain
					}
					else_if = { # Good doctors are pretty sure
						limit = {
							event_target:court_physician = {
								trigger_if = {
									limit = { physician_good_trigger = yes }
									learning >= 15
								}
								trigger_else = {
									learning >= 20
								}
							}
						}

						set_character_flag = diagnosis_is_slow_fever_flag_sure
					}
					else_if = { # Awful doctors are uncertain
						limit = {
							event_target:court_physician = {
								trigger_if = {
									limit = { physician_bad_trigger = yes }
									learning < 15
								}
								trigger_else = {
									learning < 10
								}
							}
						}

						set_character_flag = diagnosis_is_slow_fever_flag_uncertain
					}
					else = { # Otherwise, doctors are fairly sure
						set_character_flag = diagnosis_is_slow_fever_flag
					}
				}

				10 = {
					trigger = {
						trigger_if = {
							limit = { has_character_flag = correct_diagnosis }
							has_character_flag = got_camp_fever
						}
						trigger_else = {
							NOT = { has_character_flag = got_camp_fever }
						}
					}

					if = { # Horse physicians are always right in regular starts
						limit = {
							event_target:court_physician = {
								culture = horse_culture

								NOR = {
									has_alternate_start_parameter = {
										key = special_culture
										value = animal_kingdoms
									}

									has_alternate_start_parameter = {
										key = special_culture
										value = animal_world
									}
								}
							}
						}

						set_character_flag = diagnosis_is_camp_fever_flag_horse
					}
					else_if = { # Really good doctors know for sure
						limit = {
							has_character_flag = correct_diagnosis

							event_target:court_physician = {
								trigger_if = {
									limit = { physician_good_trigger = yes }
									learning >= 20
								}
								trigger_else = {
									learning >= 25
								}
							}
						}

						set_character_flag = diagnosis_is_camp_fever_flag_certain
					}
					else_if = { # Good doctors are pretty sure
						limit = {
							event_target:court_physician = {
								trigger_if = {
									limit = { physician_good_trigger = yes }
									learning >= 15
								}
								trigger_else = {
									learning >= 20
								}
							}
						}

						set_character_flag = diagnosis_is_camp_fever_flag_sure
					}
					else_if = { # Awful doctors are uncertain
						limit = {
							event_target:court_physician = {
								trigger_if = {
									limit = { physician_bad_trigger = yes }
									learning < 15
								}
								trigger_else = {
									learning < 10
								}
							}
						}

						set_character_flag = diagnosis_is_camp_fever_flag_uncertain
					}
					else = { # Otherwise, doctors are fairly sure
						set_character_flag = diagnosis_is_camp_fever_flag
					}
				}

				10 = {
					trigger = {
						trigger_if = {
							limit = { has_character_flag = correct_diagnosis }
							has_character_flag = got_bubonic_plague
						}
						trigger_else = {

						limit = {

						limit = {
							event_target:court_physician = {
								culture = horse_culture

								NOR = {
									has_alternate_start_parameter = {
										key = special_culture
										value = animal_kingdoms
									}

									has_alternate_start_parameter = {
										key = special_culture
										value = animal_world
									}
								}
							}
						}

						set_character_flag = diagnosis_is_measles_flag_horse
					}
					else_if = { # Really good doctors know for sure
						limit = {
							has_character_flag = correct_diagnosis

							event_target:court_physician = {
								trigger_if = {
									limit = { physician_good_trigger = yes }
									learning >= 20
								}
								trigger_else = {
									learning >= 25
								}
							}
						}

						set_character_flag = diagnosis_is_measles_flag_certain
					}
					else_if = { # Good doctors are pretty sure
						limit = {
							event_target:court_physician = {
								trigger_if = {
									limit = { physician_good_trigger = yes }
									learning >= 15
								}
								trigger_else = {
									learning >= 20
								}
							}
						}

					}

# Fake diagnosis - pick something!
character_event = {
	id = tianxia.1764
	desc = EVTDESC_TIANXIA_1764
	picture = GFX_evt_court_physician
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	immediate = {
		FROMFROM = {
			set_character_flag = fake_diagnosis
			
			clear_flags_with_prefix = fake_diagnosis_
			
			# Generate four options you don't actually believe in; the "sure"/etc. is how convincing you are when presentign it
			# Note that a dishonest physician that initially made an incorrect diagnosis *can* manage to accidentally be correct here; that's more fun!
			random_list = {
				10 = {
					trigger = {
						NOR = {
							has_character_flag = diagnosis_is_consumption_flag
							has_character_flag = diagnosis_is_consumption_flag_certain
							has_character_flag = diagnosis_is_consumption_flag_horse
							has_character_flag = diagnosis_is_consumption_flag_sure
							has_character_flag = diagnosis_is_consumption_flag_uncertain
						}
					}

					if = { # Horse physicians are always right in regular starts
						limit = {
							event_target:court_physician = {
								culture = horse_culture

								NOR = {
									has_alternate_start_parameter = {
										key = special_culture
										value = animal_kingdoms
									}

									has_alternate_start_parameter = {
										key = special_culture
										value = animal_world
									}
								}
							}
						}

						set_character_flag = fake_diagnosis_A_is_consumption_flag_horse
					}
					else_if = { # Really good doctors know for sure
						limit = {
							has_character_flag = correct_diagnosis

							event_target:court_physician = {
								trigger_if = {
									limit = { physician_good_trigger = yes }
									learning >= 20
								}
								trigger_else = {
									learning >= 25
								}
							}
						}

						set_character_flag = fake_diagnosis_A_is_consumption_flag_certain
					}
					else_if = { # Good doctors are pretty sure

							event_target:court_physician = {
								culture = horse_culture

								NOR = {
									has_alternate_start_parameter = {
										key = special_culture
										value = animal_kingdoms
									}

									has_alternate_start_parameter = {
										key = special_culture
										value = animal_world
									}
								}
							}
						}

						set_character_flag = fake_diagnosis_C_is_not_serious_flag_horse
					}
					else_if = { # Really good doctors know for sure
						limit = {
							has_character_flag = correct_diagnosis

							event_target:court_physician = {
								trigger_if = {
									limit = { physician_good_trigger = yes }
									learning >= 20
								}
								trigger_else = {
									learning >= 25
								}
							}
						}

						set_character_flag = fake_diagnosis_C_is_not_serious_flag_certain
					}
					else_if = { # Good doctors are pretty sure
						limit = {
							event_target:court_physician = {
								trigger_if = {
									limit = { physician_good_trigger = yes }
									learning >= 15
								}
								trigger_else = {
									learning >= 20
								}
							}
						}

						set_character_flag = fake_diagnosis_C_is_not_serious_flag_sure
					}
					else_if = { # Awful doctors are uncertain
						limit = {
							event_target:court_physician = {
								trigger_if = {
									limit = { physician_bad_trigger = yes }
									learning < 15
								}
								trigger_else = {
									learning < 10
								}
							}
						}

						set_character_flag = fake_diagnosis_C_is_not_serious_flag_uncertain
					}
					else = { # Otherwise, doctors are fairly sure
						set_character_flag = fake_diagnosis_C_is_not_serious_flag
					}
				}
			}

							has_character_flag = fake_diagnosis_C_is_pneumonia_flag_sure
							has_character_flag = fake_diagnosis_C_is_pneumonia_flag_uncertain
						}
					}

					if = { # Horse physicians are always right in regular starts
						limit = {
							event_target:court_physician = {
								culture = horse_culture

								NOR = {
									has_alternate_start_parameter = {
										key = special_culture
										value = animal_kingdoms
									}

									has_alternate_start_parameter = {
										key = special_culture
										value = animal_world
									}
								}
							}
						}

						set_character_flag = fake_diagnosis_D_is_pneumonia_flag_horse
					}
					else_if = { # Really good doctors know for sure
						limit = {
							has_character_flag = correct_diagnosis

							event_target:court_physician = {
								trigger_if = {
									limit = { physician_good_trigger = yes }
									learning >= 20
								}
								trigger_else = {
									learning >= 25
								}
							}
						}

						set_character_flag = fake_diagnosis_D_is_pneumonia_flag_certain
					}

								trigger_if = {
									limit = { physician_good_trigger = yes }
									learning >= 15
								}
								trigger_else = {
									learning >= 20
								}
							}
						}

					}
					else_if = { # Awful doctors are uncertain

							event_target:court_physician = {
								trigger_if = {
									limit = { physician_bad_trigger = yes }
									learning < 15
								}
								trigger_else = {
									learning < 10
								}
							}
						}

						set_character_flag = fake_diagnosis_D_is_pneumonia_flag_uncertain
					}

					}

# Ping sick character to ensure the Court Physician still is in a position to treat them
character_event = {
	id = tianxia.1766
	hide_window = yes
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	prisoner = no
	
	trigger = {
		character_disease_trigger = yes # Still sick
		any_courtier_or_vassal = { # With an appropriate Court Physician
			has_minor_title = title_court_physician
			OR = { # We are still expected to treat the character
				is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
				AND = {
					is_actually_incapable_trigger = no
					prisoner = no
					in_seclusion = yes
					host = {
						character = ROOT
						in_seclusion = yes
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	
	immediate = {
		# Make sure the Court Physician is the correct character, in case they were changed mid-chain
		random_courtier_or_vassal = {
			limit = {
				has_minor_title = title_court_physician
				OR = {
					vassal_of = PREV
					host = {
						character = PREV
					}
				}
			}

			save_event_target_as = court_physician
			
			character_event = { id = tianxia.1767 } # Decide on approach
		}
	}
}

# Physician approached about treatment - decide whether to try to help the character or to try to harm them
character_event = {
	id = tianxia.1767
	desc = EVTDESC_TIANXIA_1767
	picture = GFX_evt_court_physician
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTA_TIANXIA_1767 # I will treat them to the best of my ability!
		
		FROM = {
			reverse_opinion = {
				who = ROOT

# Ping to ensure the physician still can cure ROOT
character_event = {
	id = tianxia.1768
	hide_window = yes
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	prisoner = no
	
	trigger = {
		character_disease_trigger = yes # Still sick
		event_target:court_physician = { # With an appropriate Court Physician
			has_minor_title = title_court_physician
			OR = {
				vassal_of = ROOT
				host = {
					character = ROOT
				}
			}
			OR = { # We are still expected to treat the character
				is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
				AND = {
					is_actually_incapable_trigger = no
					prisoner = no
					in_seclusion = yes
					host = {
						character = ROOT
						in_seclusion = yes
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	
	immediate = {
		trigger_switch = {
			on_trigger = has_character_flag
			
			treatment_is_experimental = { event_target:court_physician = { character_event = { id = tianxia.1769 } } }
			treatment_is_mystic = { event_target:court_physician = { character_event = { id = tianxia.1770 } } }
			treatment_is_regular = { event_target:court_physician = { character_event = { id = tianxia.1771 } } }
			treatment_is_cautious = { event_target:court_physician = { character_event = { id = tianxia.1772 } } }
		}
	}
}

# Mystic treatment - pick one of the options presented
character_event = {
	id = tianxia.1770
	desc = EVTDESC_TIANXIA_1770
	picture = GFX_evt_court_physician
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion
	
	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		FROM = {
			clear_flags_with_prefix = treatment_option_
			clear_flags_with_prefix = EVTDESC_RIP_11047_
		
			# Generate up to four options
			random_list = {
				10 = {
					mult_modifier = {
						factor = 2
						event_target:court_physician = {
							OR = {
								trait = possessed
								trait = lunatic
							}
						}
					}
					set_character_flag = treatment_option_1_FLAG # Mirrors and candles
				}

				10 = {
					trigger = {
						NAND = {
							trait = paranoid
							trait = possessed
						}
						event_target:court_physician = {
							pacifist = no
							OR = {
								AND = {
									religion_group = pagan_group
									true_religion_group = pagan_group
								}
								trait = possessed
								trait = lunatic
							}
						}
					}

					set_character_flag = treatment_option_2_FLAG # Sacrificed animals, rubbed in ashes
				}

				10 = {
					trigger = {
						NOT = {
							trait = one_handed
						}
						event_target:court_physician = {
							religion = FROM
							has_secret_religion = no
						}
					}

					set_character_flag = treatment_option_3_FLAG # Conjuring, burnt fingers
				}

				10 = {
					set_character_flag = treatment_option_4_FLAG # Smoke something and become a Poet
				}

				10 = {
					trigger = {
						event_target:court_physician = {
							trait = cruel
							pacifist = no
						}
					}

					set_character_flag = treatment_option_5_FLAG # Bloodbath
				}

				5 = {
					set_character_flag = treatment_option_6_FLAG # "Special brew"
				}

				10 = {
					trigger = {
						event_target:court_physician = {
							religion = FROM
							has_secret_religion = no
						}
					}

					set_character_flag = treatment_option_7_FLAG # Walk on fire
				}

				10 = {
					trigger = {
						event_target:court_physician = {
							religion = FROM
							has_secret_religion = no
							OR = {
								trait = zealous
								trait = cruel
							}
							OR = {
								trait = lunatic
								trait = possessed
								NOR = {
									is_friend = FROM
									is_lover = FROM
									AND = {
										is_close_relative = FROM
										NOT = {
											is_rival = FROM
										}
									}
								}
							}
						}
						NOT = {
							in_seclusion = yes
						}
					}

					set_character_flag = treatment_option_8_FLAG # Physician tries to drown you; not in seclusion
				}

				5 = {
					trigger = {
						event_target:court_physician = {
							religion = FROM
							has_secret_religion = no
							pacifist = no
							OR = {
								trait = zealous
								trait = cruel
								trait = arbitrary
								trait = impaler
							}
						}

						any_courtier = {
							is_child_of = PREV
							is_ill = no
							is_abroad = no
							NOT = { trait = inbred }
							is_adult = no
							attribute_diff = {
								attribute = intrigue
								who = event_target:court_physician
								value < 0
							}
							NOR = {
								is_friend = event_target:court_physician
								is_lover = event_target:court_physician
								is_child_of = event_target:court_physician
							}
						}
						
						is_ruler = yes
					}

					random_courtier = {
						limit = {
							is_child_of = PREV
							is_ill = no
							is_abroad = no
							NOT = { trait = inbred }
							is_adult = no
							attribute_diff = {
								attribute = intrigue
								who = event_target:court_physician
								value < 0
							}
							NOR = {
								is_friend = event_target:court_physician
								is_lover = event_target:court_physician
								is_child_of = event_target:court_physician
							}
						}
						
						save_event_target_as = target_sacrifice
					}

					set_character_flag = treatment_option_9_FLAG # Physician sacrifices your child
				}

				10 = {
					trigger = {
						NOR = {
							trait = possessed
							trait = lunatic
						}
						in_seclusion = no
					}

					set_character_flag = treatment_option_10_FLAG # Am I the beast?
				}

				10 = {
					trigger = {
						event_target:court_physician = {
							pacifist = no
						}
					}
					set_character_flag = treatment_option_11_FLAG # The rat will carry your burdens
				}
				10 = {
					trigger = {
						NOR = {
							is_rival = event_target:court_physician
							event_target:court_physician = {
								has_opinion_modifier = {
									who = FROM

				is_actual_immortal_trigger = no
				OR = {
					is_friend = FROM
					is_lover = FROM
				}
				opinion = {
					who = FROM
					value >= 50
				}
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 10
				trait = depressed
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				trait = ambitious
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				trait = content
			}
		}
	}
	
	after = {
		FROM = {
			clear_flags_with_prefix = treatment_option_
			
			clr_character_flag = good_treatment_outcome
			clr_character_flag = amazing_treatment_outcome
			clr_character_flag = bad_treatment_outcome
			clr_character_flag = terrible_treatment_outcome
			
			random_list = {
				# Good
				40 = {
					mult_modifier = {
						factor = 1.2
						is_strong_trigger = yes
					}

					mult_modifier = {
						factor = 1.2
						event_target:court_physician = {
							trait = physician
							has_opinion_modifier = {
								who = PREV
								modifier = opinion_treating_to_heal
							}
						}
					}

					mult_modifier = {
						factor = 1.2
						event_target:court_physician = {
							learning >= 25
							has_opinion_modifier = {
								who = PREV
								modifier = opinion_treating_to_heal
							}
						}
					}

					mult_modifier = {
						factor = 1.2
						event_target:court_physician = {
							learning >= 20
							has_opinion_modifier = {
								who = PREV
								modifier = opinion_treating_to_heal
							}
						}
					}

					mult_modifier = {
						factor = 1.2
						event_target:court_physician = {
							learning >= 15
							has_opinion_modifier = {
								who = PREV
								modifier = opinion_treating_to_heal
							}
						}
					}

					mult_modifier = {
						factor = 0.8
						event_target:court_physician = {
							learning < 15
						}
					}

					mult_modifier = {
						factor = 0.8
						event_target:court_physician = {
							learning < 10
						}
					}

					mult_modifier = {
						factor = 0.8
						is_weak_trigger = yes
					}

					mult_modifier = {
						factor = 0.6
						event_target:court_physician = {
							physician_bad_trigger = yes
						}
					}

					mult_modifier = {
						factor = 1.2
						event_target:court_physician = {
							physician_good_trigger = yes
							has_opinion_modifier = {
								who = PREV
								modifier = opinion_treating_to_heal
							}
						}
					}

					random_list = {
						60 = { set_character_flag = good_treatment_outcome }
						40 = {
							set_character_flag = amazing_treatment_outcome
						}
					}
				}

				# Bad
				60 = {
					mult_modifier = {
						factor = 0.8
						is_strong_trigger = yes
					}

					mult_modifier = {
						factor = 0.8
						event_target:court_physician = {
							learning >= 25
							has_opinion_modifier = {
								who = PREV
								modifier = opinion_treating_to_heal
							}
						}
					}

					mult_modifier = {
						factor = 0.8
						event_target:court_physician = {
							learning >= 20
							has_opinion_modifier = {
								who = PREV
								modifier = opinion_treating_to_heal
							}
						}
					}

					mult_modifier = {
						factor = 0.8
						event_target:court_physician = {
							learning >= 15
							has_opinion_modifier = {
								who = PREV
								modifier = opinion_treating_to_heal
							}
						}
					}

					mult_modifier = {
						factor = 1.2
						event_target:court_physician = {
							learning < 15
						}
					}

					mult_modifier = {
						factor = 1.2
						event_target:court_physician = {
							learning < 10
						}
					}

					mult_modifier = {
						factor = 1.2
						is_weak_trigger = yes
					}

					mult_modifier = {
						factor = 1.4
						event_target:court_physician = {
							physician_bad_trigger = yes
						}
					}

					mult_modifier = {
						factor = 0.8
						event_target:court_physician = {
							physician_good_trigger = yes
						}
					}

					random_list = {
						50 = { set_character_flag = bad_treatment_outcome }
						50 = {
							mult_modifier = {
								factor = 4
								event_target:court_physician = {
									has_opinion_modifier = {
										who = PREV
										modifier = opinion_treating_to_harm
									}
								}
							}

							set_character_flag = terrible_treatment_outcome
						}
					}
				}
			}
			
			character_event = { id = RIP.11047 } # Actual experimental treatment event
		}
	}

# Cautious treatment - pick one of the options presented
character_event = {
	id = tianxia.1772
	desc = EVTDESC_TIANXIA_1772
	picture = GFX_evt_court_physician
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion
	
	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		FROM = {
			clear_flags_with_prefix = treatment_option_
			clear_flags_with_prefix = EVTDESC_RIP_11042_
		
			# Generate up to four options
			random_list = {
				10 = {
					mult_modifier = {
						factor = 0.1
						event_target:court_physician = {
							trait = diligent
						}
					}
					set_character_flag = treatment_option_1_FLAG # Rest in bed
				}
				10 = {
					trigger = {
						OR = {
							trait = gluttonous
							is_adult = no
						}
					}

				}

# Liege elected against treatment - inform physician
character_event = {
	id = tianxia.1773
	desc = EVTDESC_TIANXIA_1773
	picture = GFX_evt_court_physician
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	option = {
		trigger = {
			has_opinion_modifier = {
				who = FROM
				modifier = opinion_treating_to_heal
			}
		}
		
		name = EVTOPTA_TIANXIA_1773 # I see...
	}
	
	option = {
		trigger = {
			has_opinion_modifier = {
				who = FROM
				modifier = opinion_treating_to_harm
			}
		}
		
		name = EVTOPTB_TIANXIA_1773 # Did FROM suspect...?
	}
	
	after = {
		remove_opinion = {
			who = FROM
			modifier = opinion_treating_to_heal
		}
		remove_opinion = {
			who = FROM
			modifier = opinion_treating_to_harm
		}
	}
}
